# DIBY — Consolidated Master Document

**916 DIBY items** collected from every OCR workstream in this repository, in one document, in chapter → lecture → page order.

This file is generated by `tools/build_diby_master.py` from the four worker registers. It adds nothing to and removes nothing from the source registers — where a worker had not extracted a question, the text is **that worker's own raw OCR of the same page**, clearly marked as unverified. No maths was corrected, no text was re-worded.

## Where these items come from

| Source register | Items | Prompt quality |
|---|---:|---|
| `deliverables/twin-1/diby/diby_rows.json` | 143 | **verified** — visually transcribed from the rendered page (508/508 QA-PASSED scope) |
| `deliverables/twin-3-.../diby_index.csv` | 164 | 52 verified (visual-full/visual-scan) + 112 auto-extracted by dual-pass OCR |
| `ocr-output/twin-2/DIBY_Candidates.csv` | 242 | marker candidates only — draft text reconstructed from the Twin 2 page OCR |
| `deliverables/twin-4-.../diby_index.csv` | 172 | marker candidates only — draft text reconstructed from the Twin 4 transcript OCR |
| `ocr-output/sequence-series/records/*.jsonl` | 195 | automated OCR of the Sequence & Series slides — draft text pulled from the same page OCR |

## Read the labels before trusting a question

| Badge | Meaning | Items |
|---|---|---:|
| ✅ **verified** | Transcribed from the rendered page image by a human-directed visual pass. | 223 |
| 🔶 **ocr-extract** | Machine OCR extraction that passed a dual-pass check but no visual QA. | 279 |
| ⚠️ **ocr-draft** | Marker confirmed by OCR only; question text below is raw OCR, unrestored and unverified. | 348 |

Options captured for **330** items · answers/solutions shown on the slide for **537** items. Blank fields mean the source register has no value — not that the slide lacks one.

**Provenance rule used here:** printed labels, page numbers and worker statuses are reproduced exactly as the workers recorded them. Where a slide shows no printed DIBY number, the worker's internal ID is kept (`internal_id`, marked *internal — not printed on the slide*).

## Contents

- **Binomial Theorem** — 118 items · lectures 01–08 (`#binomial-theorem`)
- **Permutations & Combinations** — 158 items · lectures 01–11 (`#permutations-combinations`)
- **Quadratic Equations** — 161 items · lectures 01–13 (`#quadratic-equations`)

- **Trigonometric Functions** — 172 items · lectures 01–13 (`#trigonometric-functions`)
- **Sequence and Series** — 195 items · lectures 01–12 (`#sequence-and-series`)

**Question**

(ii) In how many ways can one drive from A to C and back to A, passing through B on both trips? (iii) In how many ways can one drive the circular trip described in (ii) without using the same road more than once.

**Options**  
(i), (ii), (iii)

**Answer as shown on the slide**  
printed [Ans.: (i) 24; (ii) 576; (iii) 360]

**Notes / uncertainty**  
duplicate of P&C L03 p37 DIBY-02; the L03 board sketches (iii) as 6 x 4 x 3 x 5 = 360. The printed (ii) is consistent with 6 x 4 x 4 x 6 = 576.
